问题：U1700 的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络？
参考答案：U1700 是 LP87702-Q1；VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 接 VCC3V3，FB_B0 接 VCC1V1，FB_B1 接 VCC1V8，VOUT_BST 接 VCC50，EN1 接 MCU_5V_EN。
实际回答：根据现有证据，关于U1700（应为LP87702 PMIC，证据18中提及）的关键引脚网络连接信息不完整。以下是基于检索结果的分析： ### 子问题1：U1700的各引脚定义是什么？哪些是关键引脚？ - **证据状态**：部分覆盖（partial） - **支持证据**： - 文档[18]（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）描述U1700采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50，并提及使能、I2C、复位等控制引脚的功能连接，但**未提供完整的引脚定义表**。 - 电路图证据（circuit:21:instance:U1700）未提供具体引脚定义。 - **缺失信息**： - U1700（LP87702）的完整引脚功能表（如引脚编号、名称、类型、描述）。 - 明确的关键引脚列表（如使能、复位、I2C、电源输入/输出等）的具体定义。 ### 子问题2：U1700每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络？ - **证据状态**：部分覆盖（partial） - **支持证据**：根据文档[18]的描述，可推断部分关键引脚的网络连接如下： - **使能引脚**：连接到MCU的P15.3引脚（文档描述：“用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出”）。 - **I2C引脚**： - SCL：连接到MCU_SCL_P15.4网络（文档描述：“MCU_SCL_P15.4”）。 - SDA：连接到MCU_SDA_P15.5网络（文档描述：“MCU_SDA_P15.5”）。 - **复位引脚**：连接到MCU的P12.1引脚（文档描述：“用MCU的P12.1引脚复位该芯片”）。 - **电源输入引脚**：连接到VCC3V3网络（文档描述：“采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”）。 - **电源输出引脚**： - VCC1V8输出：用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。 - VCC1V1输出：用于EQ6、DDR供电。 - VCC50输出：用于CAN收发器供电。 - **缺失信息**： - 电路图证据（circuit:21:instance:U1700）未提供U1700引脚的具体网络连接映射。 - 未明确哪些引脚是“关键引脚”（如COMP、GOSNS、VOSNS、EN、VIN、GND、SW、PG、MODE/SYNC、SDA、SCL、SYNC_OUT、VSEL、FSEL等）及其在电路图中的实际网络连接。 - 缺少文档文本或表格证据来确认每个引脚的确切网络名称（如引脚编号与网络名的对应关系）。 ### 证据冲突说明 - 在检索到的证据中，存在多个字段的冲突（如“ob”字段取值冲突、“none”字段取值冲突等），但均不直接涉及U1700的引脚网络连接，因此不在此处列出。 ### 总结 - 当前证据仅能根据文档描述推断部分功能引脚的网络连接（如使能、I2C、复位、电源输入/输出），但**无法提供U1700每个关键引脚（包括所有16个引脚）的完整网络连接映射**。 - **缺失项**：U1700的完整引脚功能表、所有引脚在电路图上的具体网络连接、关键引脚的明确列表。

For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | </table> | 10.4PowerSupplyRecommendations The TPS6287x-Q1 family has no special requirements for its input power supply. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. 问题：网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片？
参考答案：VCC3V3 有 52 个连接；主要 IC 至少包括 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600。
实际回答：根据电路图网表（证据[1]至[7]）和硬件设计文档（证据[8]至[15]），网络 VCC3V3（包括其分支 VCC3V3_MCU、VCC3V3_EQ、VCC3V3_ETH、VCC3V3_CAM_ACQ、VCC3V3_SW 等）上连接的主要芯片（集成电路/U 编号器件）如下： ### 1. 直接连接 VCC3V3 主干的芯片 - **U1500**（电源转换芯片，VIN 引脚接 VCC3V3） - 来源：证据[1]（U1500.VIN） - **U1600**（电源芯片，VCCIN 引脚接 VCC3V3） - 来源：证据[1]（U1600.VCCIN） - **U1700**（电源芯片，VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 接 VCC3V3） - 来源：证据[1] - **U1800**（电源芯片，VIN、MODE/SYNC 接 VCC3V3） - 来源：证据[1] - **U1802**（电源芯片，VIN 接 VCC3V3；其输出 VOUT 产生 VCC3V3_EQ） - 来源：证据[1]（U1802.VIN）、证据[4]（U1802.VOUT 在 VCC3V3_EQ 网络） - **U1804**（电源芯片，VIN 接 VCC3V3） - 来源：证据[1]（U1804.VIN） ### 2. 连接 VCC3V3_MCU 分支的芯片 - **U900C**（MCU 主芯片，VEXT、VFLEX 等引脚接 VCC3V3_MCU） - 来源：证据[6] - **U900A**（MCU 模拟/ADC 部分，AN12/EVADC 等引脚接 VCC3V3_CAM_ACQ） - 来源：证据[2] - **U1200、U1203、U1204、U1205**（DDR 或接口芯片，VI/O 引脚接 VCC3V3_MCU） - 来源：证据[6] - **U1302、U1303、U1304、U1305**（DDR 或接口芯片，VI/O 引脚接 VCC3V3_MCU） - 来源：证据[6] - **U503**（电平转换器，VCCB 引脚接 VCC3V3_MCU） - 来源：证据[6] - **Y1100**（晶振，VCC 和 std-by 接 VCC3V3_MCU） - 来源：证据[6] ### 3. 连接 VCC3V3_EQ 分支的芯片 - **U400-2**（EQ6 图像处理芯片，VDD_RING_8_0、VDD_RING_9_0 接 VCC3V3_EQ） - 来源：证据[4] - **Y500**（晶振，VDD 接 VCC3V3_EQ） - 来源：证据[4] ### 4. 连接 VCC3V3_ETH 分支的芯片 - **U1500**（同一芯片，QOD 和 VOUT 接 VCC3V3_ETH） - 来源：证据[5] - **U1501**（电源芯片或负载开关，EN 和 IN 接 VCC3V3_ETH） - 来源：证据[5] ### 5. 连接 VCC3V3_SW 分支的芯片 - **U901**（电源开关，S2 接 VCC3V3_SW） - 来源：证据[7] ### 6. 文档中补充的电源芯片（输入端均接 VCC3V3） - **LN10046**（KL30 转 VCC3V3 的主 DC-DC，对应电路图中可能为 U1500 或类似编号） - 来源：证据[8]、[9] - **TPS62872**（VCC3V3 转 VCC0V75，用于 EQ6 内核，对应电路图中可能为 U1500） - 来源：证据[9]、[14] - **SA23002**（VCC3V3 转 0V75_MIPI_DDR，用于 EQ6 IO） - 来源：证据[9]、[11] - **LP87702**（VCC3V3 转 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50，用于 EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH、CAN） - 来源：证据[9]、[12] - **TPS22918**（VCC3V3 转 VCC3V3_EQ 和 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关） - 来源：证据[9]、[14]、[15] **说明**：以上芯片的供电输入脚均直接或通过分支网络连接到 VCC3V3。电容、电阻、电感、测试点、连接器等非芯片器件未列出。所有来源均来自提供的电路图网表和 HSI 文档，不存在未覆盖的缺口。证据中冲突部分（conflicts）与问题无关，未影响结论。

Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | 唤醒电路 Wake Up circuit电源电路 power circuitKL30 EMI电路 KL30 EMI circuitKL30 转VCC3V3 KL30 to VCC3V3VCC3V3_MCU转VCC1V25_MCU VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCUVCC3V3转VCC0V75 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDRVCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 VCC3V3 to VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQVCC3V3转VCC3V3_ETH VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETHVCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQTC377最小系统 TC377 minimum systemEQ6最小系统 EQ6 minimum system摄像头最小系统 camera minimum system看门狗电路 Watchdog circuit温度采样电路 Temp Sample circuit系统内电源电压采样电源 The power System Voltage Sampling circuitETH电路 ETH circuitCAN/CAN FD电路 CAN/CAN FD circuit3V3过压保护电路 3V3 overvoltage protection circuit硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode主要用于描述当前ADAS硬件不同的运行模式（例如上电模式，下电模式，Sleep模式，运行模式等）。描述各个运行模式下的硬件模块功能，以及模式之间的切换条件。Describe the different operating mode of ADAS Hardware (e.g. power on mode, power off mode, Sleep mode，normal mode). 问题：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？它输出哪些电压，软件如何配置？
参考答案：主 PMIC 是 U1700，即 LP87702-Q1，输入 VCC3V3，输出包括 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 和 VCC50；软件配置依据 HSI 7.2 节 LP87702 配置说明，通过 I²C 相关信号配置。
实际回答：好的，根据您提供的证据，我将对您的问题进行回答。 ### 1. 给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？ 根据电路设计证据，实例U1700的器件型号为 **LP87702-Q1**，具体料号为 **LP87702RRHBRQ1**【2】。文档证据中也多次提到使用 **LP87702** PMIC方案【14】【18】【19】。 **来源**： * 电路设计图 (825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF) 中的实例U1700信息【2】。 * 文档 (600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx) 中的模块方案描述【18】【14】【19】。 ### 2. 该主PMIC输出哪些电压（各输出轨的电压值）？ 根据文档证据，该主PMIC (LP87702) 输出以下电压轨： * **VCC1V8**：用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电【18】。具体电压值为 **1.8V**。 * **VCC1V1**：用于EQ6、DDR供电【18】。具体电压值为 **1.1V**。 * **VCC50**：用于CAN收发器供电【18】。具体电压值为 **5.0V**。 **来源**： * 文档 (600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx) 中的模块方案描述【18】。 ### 3. 软件如何配置该主PMIC？ **信息缺失：** 根据现有证据，**无法确定**软件如何配置该主PMIC（例如通过I2C/SPI、寄存器设置、初始化序列）。 虽然文档明确提到“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”【16】，并且提到了通过I2C通信（使用MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5）来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能【18】，但**所有证据中均未提供Table 5-2的具体内容**，即没有提供任何具体的寄存器地址、配置值、初始化序列或配置步骤。 **缺失项**：具体的寄存器配置表、初始化序列、配置步骤等详细信息。 ### 4. 证据冲突 在您提供的证据中，存在以下需要说明的冲突情况，这些冲突主要集中在文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中： 1. **`HM ID` 字段冲突**：在同一文档中，不同的模块描述使用了不同的`HM ID`值。例如，在描述电源模块时，`HM ID` 为 `F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27`【18】；而在描述MCU最小系统模块时，`HM ID` 为 `F01`【19】。这是不同模块的标识，并非矛盾。 2. **`SM name` 字段冲突**：在不同安全机制（SM）的条目中，`SM name` 字段的值各不相同，例如 `common`、`esm`、`fusa-2316-unused`、`limitation`、`processor` 等【11】【12】【13】。这些是不同安全机制的名称，属于正常的多值情况，并非冲突。 3. **`SM TSR` 字段冲突**：不同安全机制的 `SM TSR`（技术安全要求）内容不同，例如 `after`、`ecu`、`in`、`system`、`the` 等【11】【13】。这些是不同要求的描述，并非冲突。 4. **`accuracy` 字段冲突**：在描述MCU电源需求时，文档中同时出现了 `1.2-1.35v` 和 `3.3v` 两个精度值，它们分别对应不同的电压轨（VCC1V25_MCU 和 VCC3V3_MCU）【19】。这是对不同电源轨的描述，并非冲突。 5. **`hw interface component` 字段冲突**：该字段存在 `B` 和 `qm` 两个值【11】。这可能表示不同安全等级（ASIL-B 和 QM）的硬件接口组件，但证据中未明确说明其对应关系。 **结论**：上述冲突主要是由于文档中包含了多个不同模块、不同安全机制的信息，导致同一字段在不同条目下有不同的值。这些值代表了不同的实体，而不是同一实体的矛盾信息。因此，**没有发现影响回答核心问题的直接冲突**。

There are a number of MCU SFRs that are usually programmed only once during the initialization sequence of the system or application. Modification of these SFRs during a normal application run can have a severe impact on the overall operation of the FB. Such SFRs are protected by the [Safety ENDINIT] mechanism. Any attempt to write a [Safety ENDINIT] protected SFR without deactivating the protection is discarded, the SFR is not changed, and an alarm is generated. No change order number will be allocated for the first release, and Na will be filled in the change order number.变更原因填写以客户审核需要变更为例：由于XX客户在客户审核时发现XX问题，需要调整模板。Fill in the revision note. Take the need for change in customer audit as an example: because XX customer found XX problem during customer audit, the template needs to be adjusted.变更内容填写以客户审核发现问题需要更改为例：由于XX客户在客户审核时发现XX问题，特增加XXX的变更内容。 | If the junction temperature falls below the thermal shutdown threshold of 150°C (typical), the device: · Starts switching again, starting with a new soft-start sequence · Sets the EN pin to high impedance ·Sets the PG pin to high-impedance The device clears the TSHUT bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TSHUT threshold of 150°C (typical). In a stacked configuration, in which all devices share a common enable signal, a thermal shutdown condition in one device disables the entire stack. When the hot device cools down, the whole stack automatically starts switching again. 9.3.17 Stacked Operation The user can connect multiple devices in parallel in what is known as a "stack"; for example, to increase output current capability or reduce device junction temperature. A stack comprises one primary device and one or more secondary devices. During initialization, each device monitors its SYNC_OUT pin to determine if must operate as a primary device or a secondary device: · If there is a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground, the device operates as a secondary device. · If the SYNC_OUT pin is high impedance, the device operates as a primary device. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The key points to note are: All the devices in the stack share a common enable signal, which must be pulled up with a resistance of at least 15 kQ. All the devices in the stack share a common power-good signal. All the devices in the stack share a common compensation signal. All secondary devices must connect a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground. The remote sense pins (VOSNS and GOSNS) of each device must be connected (do not leave these pins floating). Each device must be configured for the same switching frequency.· The primary device must be configured for forced PwM operation (secondary devices are automatically configured for forcedPwM operation). A stacked configuration can support synchronization to an external clock or spread-spectrum clocking.· Only the VSEL pin of the primary device is used to set the default output voltage. The VSEL pin of secondary· devices is not used and must be connected to ground. The SDA and SCL pins of secondary devices are not used and must be connected to ground. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | 10.4PowerSupplyRecommendations The TPS6287x-Q1 family has no special requirements for its input power supply. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. 问题：0.75V 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOSNS 对应 VCC0V75；配置要求见 HSI 7.3 节 TPS62872 配置。
实际回答：根据已有证据，对您的问题回答如下： ### 1. 0.75V电源由哪颗芯片产生？ **结论：** 现有证据**无法明确识别**产生0.75V电源的具体芯片型号。 **说明：** - 证据[19]（文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）中提到了“VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR”这个电源网络，并描述了MCU对此电压的ADC采样，但**未指明**该电压由哪颗芯片产生。 - 您提供的数据手册（`FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`）是关于 **TPS62870-Q1系列** 降压转换器的，该芯片可以产生0.75V输出，但证据中未明确说明该芯片就是用于产生“VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR”的电源芯片。 - 电路图（`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）中的连接信息（如Group 15、17、18等）也未直接关联到0.75V电源的产生芯片。 **结论性陈述：** 基于现有证据，无法确定0.75V电源由哪颗芯片产生。最可能的候选芯片是 **TPS62870-Q1系列**，但需要更多证据（如电路图的具体连接）来确认。 ### 2. 该芯片的输入电压是什么？ **结论：** 如果产生0.75V的芯片是**TPS62870-Q1系列**，那么其输入电压为 **2.7V 至 6.5V**。 **说明：** - 证据[10]（`FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`）中明确给出了TPS62870-Q1系列芯片的输入电压（VIN）范围：**MIN 2.7V，MAX 6.5V**。 **结论性陈述：** 如果使用TPS62870-Q1系列芯片，输入电压范围是2.7V至6.5V。但再次强调，芯片型号未确认，因此此输入电压信息为条件性结论。 ### 3. 文档对该芯片有什么配置要求？ **结论：** 如果产生0.75V的芯片是**TPS62870-Q1系列**，文档中提出了多种配置要求，具体如下： **说明：** - **输出范围设置：** 证据[13]指出，可通过`VRANGE[1:0]`位选择输出范围，默认范围为0.4V至1.675V，步进5mV。更改此位后必须重新写入`VSET`寄存器。 - **输出值设置：** 证据[13]指出，输出值由`VSET[7:0]`位控制，软启动过程中更改的值会被忽略，直至软启动完成。 - **软启动时间：** 证据[16]和[17]提到了`SSTIME[1:0]`位用于设置软启动时间，默认为1ms。 - **Hiccup模式：** 证据[11]指出，可通过`CONTROL1`寄存器的`HICCUPEN`位启用或禁用Hiccup模式。证据冲突显示，关于该位的值存在冲突（应为0或1），请见下方冲突说明。 - **开关频率：** 证据[16]指出，开关频率由`FSEL`引脚的外部电阻决定。 - **引脚状态：** 证据[15]（文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）中提到了对未使用GPIO引脚、JTAG端口等引脚状态的配置要求（如内部上拉/下拉），但这些是针对**MCU**（如TC3XX）的配置，而非电源芯片本身。 **结论性陈述：** 如果使用TPS62870-Q1系列芯片，其配置要求包括输出范围、输出值、软启动时间、Hiccup模式、开关频率等，具体值需参考数据手册。 ### 4. 证据冲突说明 系统检测到以下证据冲突，**无法合并**为一个确定结论： - **字段：`ensure that hiccupen`**：关于TPS62870-Q1系列芯片的`HICCUPEN`寄存器位，证据[11]中出现了两个相互矛盾的取值：`0` 和 `1`。这表明不同证据对该位的推荐值存在分歧，因此无法确定正确的配置是启用还是禁用Hiccup模式。 - **其他冲突**：关于`id`、`sm name`、`sm tsr`、`note`、`sri`、`lsb`、`vih`、`lol`、`unnamed`等字段的冲突，主要来源于文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`和`FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`，这些冲突不影响0.75V电源识别问题的核心结论，但表明文档中存在不一致或错误。 ### 缺失信息总结 1. **产生0.75V电源的芯片具体型号。** 这是回答所有问题的前提。 2. **确认该芯片型号的电路原理图或物料清单（BOM）信息。** 3. **解决TPS62870-Q1系列芯片`HICCUPEN`配置位的证据冲突，以确定正确的配置值。**

If hiccup operation is enabled and the high-side switch current exceeds the current limit threshold on 32 consecutive switching cycles, the device: Stops switching for 128 clock cycles, after which it automatically starts switching again (it starts a new soft-start sequence) · Sets the HICCUP bit in the STATUS register Pulls the PG pin low. The PG pin stays low until the overload condition goes away and the device can start up correctly and regulate the output voltage. Note that power-good function has a deglitch circuit, which delays the rising edge of the power-good signal by 40 μs (typical). Hiccup operation continues in a repeating sequence of 32 cycles in current limit, followed by a pause of 128 cycles, followed by a soft-start attempt for as long as the output overload condition exists. The device clears the HICCUP bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition has been removed. 9.3.13.3CurrentLimitMode To enable current limit mode, ensure that HICCUPEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. When current limit operation is enabled, the device limits the high-side switch current cycle-by-cycle for as long as the overload condition exists. If the device limits the high-side switch current for four or more consecutive switching cycles, the device sets ILIM = 1 in the STATUS register. The device clears the ILIM bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition no longer exits. 9.3.14 Power Good (PG) The power good (PG) pin is bidirectional and has two functions: · In a standalone configuration and in the primary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an open- drain output that indicates the status of the converter or stack. · In a secondary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an input that indicates when the soft-start sequence is complete and all converters in the stack can change from DCM switching to CCM switching. 9.3.14.1 Standalone orPrimaryDeviceBehavior The primary purpose of the PG pin is to indicate if the output voltage is in regulation, but it also indicates if the device is in thermal shutdown or disabled. Table 9-7 summarizes the behavior of the PG pin in a standalone or primary device. | Unnamed: 0：SM[HW]:SRI:SWAP_CONFIGURATION_PROTECTION; Unnamed: 1：6.479; Unnamed: 2：The safety mechanism checks for unintentional SWAP configuration change. The VRANGE[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register control which range is active (see Table 9-3). The default output voltage range after device initialization is 0.4 V to 1.675 V in 5-mV steps. Note that every change to the VRANGE[1:0] bits must be followed by a write to the VSET register, even if the value of the VSET[7:o] bits does not change. This sequence is required for the device to start to use the new voltage range. Also note that the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range uses a 0.8-V reference and the other ranges use a 0.4-V reference. Switching to and from the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range can therefore cause increased output voltage undershoot or overshoot while the device switches its internal reference. In device variants that do not have I?C, the output voltage range is factory-set to 0.4 V to 1.675 V. 9.3.6.2OutputVoltageSetpoint Together with the selected range, the VSET[7:0] bits in the VSET register control the output voltage setpoint of the device (see Table 9-4). During initialization, the device reads the state of the VSEL pin and selects the default output voltage according to Table 9-5. Note that the VSEL pin also selects the I?C target address of the device (see Table 9-10). If the user programs new output voltage setpoint (VOUT[7:0]), output voltage range (VRANGE[1:0]), or soft-start time (SSTIME[1:o]) settings when the device has already begun its soft-start sequence, the device ignores the new values until the soft-start sequence is complete. If the user changes the value of VSET[7:0] during soft start, the device first ramps to the value that VSET[7:0] had when the soft-start sequence began. Then, when soft start is complete, ramps up or down to the new value. If the user changes VOUT[7:0], VRAMP[1:0], or SSTIME[1:0] while EN is low, the device uses the new values the next time the user enables it. | Unnamed: 0：SM[HW]:SRI:SAFETY_ENDINIT; Unnamed: 1：6.475; Unnamed: 2：MCU End of Safe Initialization (Safety ENDINIT) Protection. Modification of these SFRs during a normal application run can have a severe impact on the overall operation of the FB. Such SFRs are protected by the [Safety ENDINIT] mechanism. Any attempt to write a [Safety ENDINIT] protected SFR without deactivating the protection is discarded, the SFR is not changed, and an alarm is generated. None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | window comparator monitors the output voltage, and the output of the comparator goes high if the output voltage is either less than 95% (typical) or greater than 105% (typical) of the nominal output voltage. The output of the window comparator is deglitched - the typical deglitch time is 40 μs - and then used to drive the open-drain PG pin. During DVS activity, when the DC/DC converter transitions from one output voltage setting to another, the output voltage can temporarily exceed the limits of the window comparator and pull the PG pin low. The device has a feature to disable this behavior. If PGBLNKDVS = 1 in the CONTROL3 register, the device ignores the output of the power-good window comparator while DvS is active. Note that the PG pin is always low, regardless of the output of the window comparator, when: · The device is in thermal shutdown. The device is disabled. The device is in undervoltage lockout.· The device is in soft start. 9.3.14.2 Secondary Device Behavior initialization, the device presets FF1 and FF2, which pulls down the PG pin and forces the device to operate in DCM. When the device completes its soft start, it resets FF2, which turns off Q1. However, in a stacked configuration, all devices share the same PG signal, and therefore the PG pin stays low until all devices in the stack have completed their soft start. When that happens, FF1 is reset and the converters operate in CCM. 9.3.15 Remote Sense The device has two pins, VOSNS and GOSNS, to remotely sense the output voltage. Remote sensing lets the converter sense the output voltage directly at the point-of-load and increases the accuracy of the output voltage regulation. 9.3.16 Thermal Warning and Shutdown The device has a two-level overtemperature detection function. If the junction temperature rises above the thermal warning threshold of 150°C (typical), the device sets the TWARN bit in the STATUS register. The device clears the TWARN bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TWARN threshold of 130°C (typical). If the junction temperature rises above the thermal shutdown threshold of 170°C (typical), the device: · Stops switching · Pulls down the EN pin (if SINGLE = 0 in the CONTROL3 register) · Enables the output discharge (if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register) ·Sets the TSHUT bit in the STATUS register · Pulls the PG pin low | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. A valid I2C address selects the target. The target performs an update on the falling edge of the acknowledge signal that follows the LSB byte. Then, when the user pulls the EN pin high, the device starts up with the desired start-up voltage. Note that if the user changes the device settings through I?C while the device is ramping, the device ignores the changes until the ramp is complete 9.3.6.4 Dynamic Voltage Scaling If the user changes the output voltage setpoint while the DC/DC converter is operating, the device ramps up or down to the new voltage setting in a controlled way. The VRAMP[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register sets the slew rate when the device ramps from one voltage to another during DVS (see Table 9-6). Note that ramping the output to a higher voltage requires additional output current, so that during DvS, the converter must generate a total output current given by: dVour lour = lour c) + Cour dVOouT where · lout is the total current the converter must generate while ramping to a higher voltage. louT(Dc) is the DC load current.· Cout is the total output capacitance. dVout/dt is the slew rate of the output voltage (programmable in the range 0.5 mV/μs to 10 mV/us). For correct operation, ensure that the total output current during DVS does not exceed the current limit of the device. 9.3.7 Compensation (CoMP) The CoMP pin is the connection point for an external compensation network. A series-connected resistor and capacitor to GOSNS is sufficient for typical applications. The series-connected resistor also provides enough scope to optimize the loop response for a wide range of operating conditions. When using multiple devices in a stacked configuration, all devices share a common compensation network, and the COMP pin makes sure there is equal current sharing between them (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.8 ModeSelection and Clock Synchronization (MODE/SYNC) A high level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects forced PWM operation. A low level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects power save operation, in which, the device automatically transitions between PWM and PFM, according to the load conditions. | Figure 10-12. The MODE/SYNC pin is logically ORed with the FPWMEN bit in the CONTROL1 register (see Table 9-1). When multiple devices are used together in a stacked configuration, the MODE/SYNC pin of the secondary devices is the input for the clock signal (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.9 Spread Spectrum Clocking (SSC) The device has a spread spectrum clocking function that can reduce electromagnetic interference (EMl). When the SSC function is active, the device modulates the switching frequency to approximately ±10% the nominal value. The frequency modulation has a triangular characteristic (see Figure 9-11). To use the SSC function, make sure that: · SSCEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. · Forced PWM operation is selected (MODE pin is high or FPWMEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register). Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 ·The device is not synchronized to an external clock. To disable the SSC function, ensure that SCCEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. To use the SSC function with multiple devices in a stacked configuration, ensure that the primary converter runs from its internal oscillator and synchronize all secondary converters to the primary clock (see Figure 9-14). 9.3.10 Output Discharge The device has an output discharge function that ensures a defined ramp down of the output voltage when the device is disabled and keeps the output voltage close to 0 V while the device is off. The output discharge function is enabled when DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The output discharge function is enabled by default. If enabled, the device discharges the output under the following conditions: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = O in the CONTROL1 register. · A thermal shutdown event occurs. · A UVLO event occurs. · An OVLO event occurs. The output discharge function is not available until the user has enabled the device at least once after power up During power down, the device continues to discharge the output for as long as the internal supply voltage is greater than approximately 1.8 V. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F17</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH is used for ETH power supply, and the load switching scheme is now selected.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3_ETH，提供给MCU的ETH供电。P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 The TPS22918 load switch is used to convert the VCC3V3 system power to VCC3V3-ETH to supply power to ETH of the MCU. 问题：3.3V 电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？
参考答案：HSI 2.3.12 节声明存在 3V3 过压保护电路；EDF 只能用于说明观察到的二极管或 TVS 连接拓扑，不能在没有数据手册证据时断言具体阈值或保护能力。
实际回答：好的，这是基于给定证据的分析结果。 ### 3.3V电源过压保护分析 根据提供的证据，3.3V电源轨存在过压保护，其实现方式可以总结为以下两点： #### 1. 是否存在过压保护？ (Sub-question 1) **结论：是，存在。** * **来源说明**： * 文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`中的模块`F18`明确命名为“3V3过压保护电路”(3V3 overvoltage protection circuit) [9]。 * 同一文档的模块`F18`描述中明确指出，AOU（客户/主机厂）要求MCU需要硬件外部增加过压保护电路对`VCC3V3_MCU`进行电压监控[5]。 * 系统功能模块列表中也明确包含了“3V3过压保护电路”这一项[9]。 #### 2. 如何实现过压保护？ (Sub-question 2) **结论：通过一个由可控精密稳压源、NMOS和PMOS组成的硬件电路，以及电源输入端的TVS管共同实现。** * **保护电路实现细节（针对MCU的VCC3V3_MCU）**： * **来源说明**：文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`中的模块`F18`详细描述了针对`VCC3V3_MCU`的过压保护电路方案[5]。 * **工作原理**： 1. **正常工作时**：当`VCC3V3`电压在NMOS的开启阈值（`VGS`）和4.03V之间时，NMOS和PMOS导通，`VCC3V3_SW`（即受保护的输出）与`VCC3V3`直通。 2. **过压保护时**：当`VCC3V3`电压超过4.03V时，电路中的可控精密稳压源（通过其cathode引脚）输出逻辑低电平，关闭NMOS和PMOS，从而切断`VCC3V3_SW`的输出，对后级负载起到过压保护作用[5]。 3. **欠压状态**：当`VCC3V3`电压低于4.03V且低于NMOS的开启阈值时，NMOS和PMOS关闭，`VCC3V3_SW`无电[5]。 * **注意**：该保护阈值设定为4.03V。 * **瞬态过压保护（TVS管）**： * **来源说明**：原理图文件`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`中，在`VCC3V3_MCU`网络（ID: 21）上，观察到了多个TVS管拓扑，包括`D1200`, `D1201`, `D1300`, `D1301`, `D1400`, `D1401`[1]。 * **作用**：这些TVS管用于吸收瞬态过压脉冲（如ESD或浪涌），为3.3V电源网络提供保护。需要注意的是，这些TVS管是作为保护元件存在于电路中的，但具体型号和参数在此证据中未提供。 ### 缺失信息 * **保护电路的具体元器件型号**：文档中虽描述了`F18`模块的电路原理，但未明确提及所使用的具体元器件（如可控精密稳压源、NMOS、PMOS的型号）。原理图证据中虽有TVS管，但未列出其具体型号和参数。 * **保护阈值的详细来源**：文档中给出的4.03V阈值，其设定的详细依据或计算过程未提供。 * **具体连接和网络**：`F18`模块的电路图（模块框图）在证据中为空，因此无法验证其具体的网络连接和信号走向。 ### 证据冲突 在证据中检测到以下冲突，但**这些冲突与3.3V电源的过压保护问题无关**，它们分别源于MCU电源需求描述和`TPS62872-Q1`数据手册中关于不同引脚或不同测试条件的参数描述： 1. **关于MCU的VCC1V25_MCU电源精度**：在文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`的`F01`模块中，`VCC1V25_MCU`的精度描述在不同位置出现不一致，一处写为“1.2-1.35V”，另一处写为“3.3V”。这属于数据录入错误，与3.3V电源的过压保护无关。 2. **关于`TPS62872-Q1`的`SDA`引脚参数**：在`FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`（`TPS62872-Q1`数据手册）中，关于`SDA`引脚的低电平输出电压（`VoL`）参数，在不同测试条件下（`IoL = 3 mA`, `5 mA`, `9 mA`）给出了不同的典型值。这属于同一性能参数在不同条件下的正常表现，并非冲突。 3. **关于`TPS62872-Q1`的`SYNC_OUT`引脚输入电流**：在同一份数据手册中，对`SYNC_OUT`引脚的高电平输入电流（`IIH`）给出了两个不同的测试条件（`Vih = 2 V` vs `Vih = VIN`）和参数值。 **结论**：3.3V电源轨确实存在过压保护，其实现方式主要依赖一个特定的硬件电路（设定阈值4.03V）和输入侧的TVS管。保护电路的具体细节在文档中有描述，但缺少元器件型号和详细的电路连接图。

The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | 唤醒电路 Wake Up circuit电源电路 power circuitKL30 EMI电路 KL30 EMI circuitKL30 转VCC3V3 KL30 to VCC3V3VCC3V3_MCU转VCC1V25_MCU VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCUVCC3V3转VCC0V75 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDRVCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 VCC3V3 to VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQVCC3V3转VCC3V3_ETH VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETHVCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQTC377最小系统 TC377 minimum systemEQ6最小系统 EQ6 minimum system摄像头最小系统 camera minimum system看门狗电路 Watchdog circuit温度采样电路 Temp Sample circuit系统内电源电压采样电源 The power System Voltage Sampling circuitETH电路 ETH circuitCAN/CAN FD电路 CAN/CAN FD circuit3V3过压保护电路 3V3 overvoltage protection circuit硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode主要用于描述当前ADAS硬件不同的运行模式（例如上电模式，下电模式，Sleep模式，运行模式等）。描述各个运行模式下的硬件模块功能，以及模式之间的切换条件。Describe the different operating mode of ADAS Hardware (e.g. power on mode, power off mode, Sleep mode，normal mode). </td><td></td><td >92</td><td >94</td><td >96</td><td >%VoUT </td></tr> <tr><td >VT+</td><td > Positive-going power good threshold voltage (output overvoltage)</td><td></td><td >104</td><td >106</td><td >108</td><td >%VouT</td></tr> <tr><td >VT-</td><td >Negative-going power good threshold voltage (output overvoltage)</td><td></td><td >102</td><td >104</td><td >106</td><td >%VoUT </td></tr> <tr><th >VoL</th><th >Low-level output voltage (PG)</th><th >loL = 1 mA</th><th></th><th></th><th >0.3</th><th></th></tr> </table> | 9.3.11 Undervoltage Lockout (UVLO) The device has an undervoltage lockout function that disables the device if the supply voltage is too low for correct operation. The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. The current limit in practice is given by: where: · I, is the inductor current. ·ILiMH is the high-side current limit threshold measured at DC Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | The diagram shows a circuit layout with two labeled latches: FF1 at the top and FF2 at the bottom. FF1 is connected to an “Input Buffer” block on its right, which has text below it reading “V_IL = < 0.4 V” and “V_IH = > 0.8 V”. Page Changes from Revision A (July 2022) to Revision B (January 2023) | Figure 10-1. Typical ApplicationSchematic 110 nH±20% 0.75V V VIN SW 3.3V VIN MODE/SYNC 20 kQ C C VoO Load VOSNS EN GOSNS SDA SCL >Rz VSEL FSEL 3.3V Cc 6.2 kQ [cc M10 kQ COMP 1kQ OPG PG SYNC_OUT GND 10pF GND | A stacked configuration uses a daisy-chained clocking signal, in which each device switches with a phase offset of approximately 140° relative to the adjacent devices in the daisy-chain. To daisy-chain the clocking signal, connect the SYNC_OUT pin of the primary device to the MODE/SYNC pin of the first secondary device. Connect the SYNC_OUT pin of the first secondary device to the MODE/SYNC pin of the second secondary device. Continue this connection scheme for all devices in the stack, to daisy-chain them together. Hiccup overcurrent protection must not be used in a stacked configuration. In a stacked configuration, the common enable signal also acts as a SYSTEM_READY signal (see Section 9.3.3). Each device in the stack can pull its EN pin low during device start-up or when a fault occurs. Thus, the stack is only enabled when all devices have completed their start-up sequence and are fault-free. A fault in any one device disables the whole stack for as long as the fault condition exists. During start-up, the primary converter pulls the COMP pin low for as long as the enable signal (SYSTEM_READY) is low. When the enable signal goes high, the primary device actively controls the COMP pin and all converters in the stack follow the COMP voltage. During start-up, each device in the stack pulls its PG pin low while it initializes. When initialization is complete, each secondary device in the stack sets its PG pin to a high impedance and the primary device alone controls the state of the PG signal. The PG pin goes high when the stack has completed its start-up ramp and the output voltage is within specification. The secondary converters in the stack detect the rising edge of the power-good signal and switch from DCM operation to CCM operation. After the stack has successfully started up, the primary device controls the power-good signal in the normal way. In a stacked configuration, there are some faults that only affect individual devices, and other faults that affect all devices. For example, if one device enters current limit, only that device is affected. But a thermal shutdown or undervoltage lockout event in one device disables all devices through the shared enable (SYSTEM_READY) signal. Functionality During Stacked Operation Some device features are not available during stacked operation, or are only available in the primary converter. Fault Handling During Stacked Operation In a stacked configuration, there are some faults that only affect individual devices and other faults that affect all devices. For example, if one device enters current limit, only that device is affected. A thermal shutdown or undervoltage lockout event in one device disables all devices through the shared enable (SYSTEM_READY) signal. Table 9-9 summarizes the fault handling of the TPS6287x-Q1 devices during stacked operation.

问题：板上用 TPS22918 做了哪些电源开关？逐个给出输入、输出和使能。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8 到 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：好的，根据您提供的证据，我将严格基于已有信息回答您的问题，并明确指出信息缺失的部分。 ### 子问题1：电路板上所有使用 TPS22918 芯片的电源开关实例有哪些？ **回答：** 根据提供的文档证据，电路板上共有 **3个** 使用 TPS22918 芯片的电源开关实例，分别对应模块 **F16**、**F17** 和 **F19**。 * **来源说明：** * 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中的模块列表明确将 F16、F17、F19 标识为使用 TPS22918 负载开关【10†L17-L19】。 * 该文档中关于 F16、F17、F19 的详细描述也确认了 TPS22918 的使用【15†L5】【11†L8】【19†L8】。 ### 子问题2：对于每个 TPS22918 实例，其输入（VIN）、输出（VOUT）和使能（EN）分别连接到哪些网络？ **回答：** **此部分信息缺失严重。** 现有证据仅能提供每个实例的输入和输出电源的网络名称，以及使能控制信号的名称，**但无法确定每个实例的输入、输出和使能引脚在电路原理图中具体连接到的网络名称**。特别是，使能（EN）引脚所连接的具体网络名称在证据中未被直接给出。以下是根据文档证据整理出的部分信息： * **实例1：模块 F16** * **输入（VIN）：** 文档描述为“VCC3V3”【10†L17】。 * **输出（VOUT）：** 文档描述为“VCC3V3_EQ”【10†L17】。 * **使能（EN）：** 文档描述为由 MCU 的 **P20.3** 引脚控制，使能信号名称为 **MCU_3V3_EQ_EN**【13†L4】。但未提供该信号在电路中连接到 TPS22918 的 EN 引脚的具体网络名称。 * **实例2：模块 F17** * **输入（VIN）：** 文档描述为“VCC3V3”【10†L18】。 * **输出（VOUT）：** 文档描述为“VCC3V3_ETH”【10†L18】。 * **使能（EN）：** 文档描述为由 MCU 的 **P21.0** 引脚控制，使能信号名称为 **MCU_3V3_ETH_EN**【13†L12】。但未提供该信号在电路中连接到 TPS22918 的 EN 引脚的具体网络名称。 * **实例3：模块 F19** * **输入（VIN）：** 文档描述为“VCC1V8”【10†L19】。 * **输出（VOUT）：** 文档描述为“VCC1V8_EQ”【10†L19】。 * **使能（EN）：** 文档描述为由 MCU 的 **P20.0** 引脚控制，使能信号名称为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**【13†L17】。但未提供该信号在电路中连接到 TPS22918 的 EN 引脚的具体网络名称。 ### 缺失信息汇总 1. **子问题2信息缺失：** 对于上述三个 TPS22918 实例，现有证据**缺少**以下信息： * **引脚连接网络：** 每个实例的输入（VIN）、输出（VOUT）和使能（EN）引脚在电路原理图中具体连接到的网络名称（例如，`VCC3V3` 是一个电源轨描述，但其具体的PCB网络名未提供）。 * **使能信号网络：** 使能引脚（EN）所连接的具体网络名称（例如，虽然知道了使能信号名称为`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN`，但它在电路中连接TPS22918 EN引脚的网络名称未知）。 ### 证据冲突说明 在提供的证据中，存在与**TPS22918无关**的冲突，特此说明，但不影响对您问题的回答。 * **冲突内容：** 在文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中，关于模块 F16 的使能引脚描述，不同证据片段给出了不一致的取值【13†L4】【15†L7】。这些冲突值（如“P20.3”和“P20.3/...”）是用于描述MCU引脚的复用功能，并非描述TPS22918芯片本身，因此不构成实质冲突。

KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. 问题：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？
参考答案：供电链路为 VCC3V3 → U1500（TPS22918，ON=MCU_3V3_ETH_EN）→ VCC3V3_ETH → U1501（TPS74501 LDO）→ VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：根据检索到的证据，以太网PHY的1.0V电源（VCC1V0_ETH）的供电级联路径如下： ### 1. 以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源需求规格 * **电压范围**：证据表明，MCU采样以太网的VCC1V0_ETH电源电压，精度要求为 **0.9V ~ 1.1V**。此精度要求可作为电源设计参考。 [19] * **电流规格**：**证据缺失**。检索到的证据中未提及以太网PHY的1.0V电源的具体电流需求。 ### 2. 1.0V 电源的上级电源 * **上级电源**：1.0V电源（VCC1V0_ETH）的上级电源是 **VCC3V3_ETH**。 [5] * **电压转换来源**：VCC3V3_ETH由系统主电源轨 **VCC3V3** 转换而来。具体路径为：**系统12V电池电压 → 一级DC-DC转换为VCC3V3 → 负载开关（Load Switch）转换为VCC3V3_ETH**。该负载开关由MCU的P21.0引脚使能。 [18] ### 3. 1.0V 电源的供电级联路径 * **一级转换 (VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH)**：通过一个负载开关（Load Switch）实现，用于为以太网（ETH）供电。 [18] * **二级转换 (VCC3V3_ETH to VCC1V0_ETH)**：通过一个型号为 **TPS74501** 的稳压器（U1501）实现。该稳压器将VCC3V3_ETH转换为VCC1V0_ETH。 [5, 13] * **最终输出**：TPS74501的输出引脚（U1501.OUT）连接到网络 **VCC1V0_ETH**，该网络再通过电感（L1403）等滤波元件为以太网PHY芯片（U1400）供电。 [5] ### 证据冲突 * 在“HW Interface Component”字段中，不同证据对同一功能模块（如“F09/ETH and CAN power samp2”）的归属存在冲突，取值分别为“QM”和“B”。这影响了部分模块的功能安全等级判断，但未直接涉及1.0V电源的供电路径，因此对主回答无影响。 [19, 20] ### 缺失信息 1. **以太网PHY 1.0V电源的电流需求规格**：未检索到相关数据。 2. **第二级转换器（U1501，TPS74501）的输入电源网络**：证据显示其输出连接到VCC1V0_ETH，但其输入引脚的具体连接网络（如VCC3V3_ETH）在检索到的电路连接信息中未明确列出，因此无法100%确认其输入电源，尽管从逻辑上推断为VCC3V3_ETH。 3. **具体滤波网络**：证据显示VCC1V0_ETH网络连接了多个电容和电感，但其具体参数和拓扑结构细节未提供。

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | In standalone (nonstacked) applications, the user can disable the active pulldown of the EN pin if the user sets SINGLE = 1 in the CONTROL3 register. Fault conditions have no effect on the EN pin when SINGLE = 1 (the EN pin is always pulled down during device initialization). In stacked applications, ensure that SINGLE = 0. When the internal SYSTEM_READY signal is low (that is, initialization is complete and there are no fault conditions), the internal open-drain transistor is high impedance and the EN pin functions like a standard input. A high level on the EN pin enables the DC/DC converter in the device. A low level disables the DC/DC converter (the I2C interface is enabled as soon as the device has completed its initialization and is not affected by the state of the internal ENABLE or SYSTEM_READY signals). A low level on the EN pin forces the device into shutdown. During shutdown, the MOSFETs in the power stage are off, the internal control circuitry is disabled, and the device consumes only 20 μA (typical). The rising threshold voltage of the EN pin is 1.0 V and the falling threshold voltage is 0.9 V. The tolerance of the threshold voltages is ±30 mV, which means that the user can use the EN pin to implement precise turn-on and turn-off behavior. When power is applied to the VIN pin, the toggling of the EN pin does not reset the loaded default register settings. 9.3.4 Start-Up When the voltage on the VIN pin exceeds the positive-going UVLO threshold, the device initializes as follows: ·The device pulls the EN pin low. ·The device enables the internal reference voltage. · The device reads the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. · The device loads the default values into the device registers. When initialization is complete, the device enables I²C communication and releases the EN pin. The external circuitry controlling the EN pin now determines the behavior of the device: · If the EN pin is low, the device is disabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers, but the DC/DC converter does not operate · If the EN pin is high, the device is enabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers and, after a short delay, the DC/DC converter starts to ramp up its output. 检索上下文：Instance U1700: LP87702-Q1, 20200424007, LP87702RRHBRQ1, pqfn50p5x5x90-32. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN NRST电压低于阈值。LP87702-Q1器件停止工作。 READ OTP 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO电平，并且NRST电压高于阈值电平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平和偏置电路被启用。OTP配置被加载到寄存器。 STANDBY 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平、控制和偏压电路被启用。所有的寄存器都可以被主机处理器通过系统串口读写。电压转换器可以根据需要进行使能输出。 ACTIVE 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。稳压DC/DC变换器处于使能状态。所有的寄存器都可以被MCU通过系统串口读写。 SHUTDOWN After the enable signal MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L, the secondary power stops the 0V75_MIPI_DDR power supply. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. 问题：主控 MCU 是哪颗，型号是什么？
参考答案：EDF 中 U900 为 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，即 AURIX TC367；HSI 章节名称写作 TC377 最小系统。回答应披露 TC367 与 TC377 的来源差异，不能合并为无冲突的单一型号。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，主控MCU的型号为 **SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**。 **来源说明**： - 证据[1]（文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）中明确说明：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”。 - 证据[5]（同一文档）中“F01 MCU”模块的功能定义列为“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。 **缺失信息**：无。 **证据冲突**：在提供的冲突列表中，未涉及主控MCU型号的冲突，因此无需额外说明。
检索上下文：<table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | Sheet名称：0_Instruction Manual; 内容摘要：使用说明; 项目适配说明：nan ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：1_Face; 内容摘要：文档封面页; 问题：U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络分别是什么？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 为 VCC3V3，VOSNS 为 VCC0V75，EN 接 N19888813，并通过连接到 MCU 的 SDA/SCL 进行 I²C 控制。
实际回答：好的，作为专业的硬件资料检索助手，我将严格基于您提供的证据，回答关于U1800的输入、输出检测、使能和I²C控制网络的问题。 **核心结论：** 根据现有证据，U1800（型号为TPS62872）是一个将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75的电源转换芯片，为EQ6L的内核供电。其使能控制由MCU的GPIO引脚实现，I²C控制网络连接到MCU的I²C总线。关于其具体的输入引脚定义和输出检测功能，现有证据不完整。 --- ### 针对您问题的详细回答（按子问题组织）： #### 1. U1800的输入引脚或信号定义是什么？ * **证据描述**：证据[1]和[20]指出，U1800（TPS62872）的输入是“VCC3V3”，输出是“VCC0V75”。它是“VCC3V3转VCC0V75”的电源转换芯片。 * **来源**：`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`（证据ID: 7a6ae2179c8765b3, 证据ID: b6943ae371d76077）。 * **缺失信息**：**证据缺失**。现有证据未提供U1800芯片的具体引脚编号（如Pin 1, Pin 2等）及其对应信号的定义（如VIN, EN, PG等）。电路图`circuit:21:instance:U1800`虽被检索到，但其内容未在提供的证据中显示。因此，**无法确定U1800的详细输入引脚定义**。 #### 2. U1800的输出检测功能是什么？ * **证据描述**：证据[12]提到，系统要求MCU对U1800（TPS62872）的输出电压VCC0V75进行ADC采样和异常监控。具体采样引脚为AN19。 * **来源**：`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`（证据ID: f4318d00b223c30b）。 * **缺失信息**：**证据缺失**。现有证据明确了**系统对VCC0V75的监控需求**，但未明确说明U1800芯片本身是否具有“输出检测”功能（如Power Good引脚）。U1800芯片的datasheet（`FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`，证据ID: a8815b0671c5a0ec）也仅提供了I²C地址和配置信息，未描述其输出检测功能。因此，**U1800自身的输出检测功能（如果有）是未知的**。 #### 3. U1800的使能控制信号是什么？ * **证据描述**：证据[1]和[20]明确指出，U1800（TPS62872）由MCU的P22.0引脚进行使能和关断控制。该信号名为`MCU_VCC0V75_EN`。 * 信号高电平：使能VCC0V75电源。 * 信号低电平：关断VCC0V75电源。 * 未使能时状态：HI-Z，硬件外部下拉。 * **来源**：`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`（证据ID: 7a6ae2179c8765b3, 证据ID: b6943ae371d76077）。 * **结论**：U1800的使能控制信号是来自MCU引脚P22.0的`MCU_VCC0V75_EN`。 #### 4. U1800的I²C控制网络配置或连接方式是什么？ * **证据描述**：证据[20]明确指出，U1800（TPS62872）通过I²C通信来设置输出电压和进行通信。该I²C网络连接到MCU的引脚`MCU_SDA_P15.5`（SDA线）和`MCU_SCL_P15.4`（SCL线）。 * **来源**：`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`（证据ID: b6943ae371d76077）。 * **I²C地址**：证据[13]（U1800的datasheet）显示，型号为`TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1`的器件，其I²C地址为`0x42`，默认启动电压为0.875V。 * **结论**：U1800的I²C控制网络连接到MCU的I²C总线（SDA: P15.5, SCL: P15.4），其I²C地址为0x42。 --- ### 证据冲突说明 根据检索账本，存在以下证据冲突，但**这些冲突与U1800的具体问题不直接相关**，因此不影响上述结论： * **冲突字段**：`hw interface component`、`none`、`mcu component`、`ecu connector`、`default setting`、`gpioxx gpioyy`。 * **冲突原因**：不同来源的文档（主要是`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）对同一字段给出了不同取值。例如，`hw interface component`字段同时存在`asil`、`b`、`qm`等值，`ecu connector`字段同时存在`pin`、`x1900-1`、`x2000-54`等值。 * **说明**：这些冲突值出现在文档中，但未明确关联到U1800组件，且与当前问题的核心（U1800的引脚定义、使能、I²C）不直接相关，因此不影响对U1800的上述分析。 ### 总结 | 功能 | 结论 | 来源 | 备注 | | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | | **输入** | VCC3V3 | 证据[1], [20] | **具体引脚定义缺失** | | **输出监控** | 系统对输出电压VCC0V75进行ADC采样（AN19） | 证据[12] | **U1800芯片自身的输出检测功能未知** | | **使能控制** | MCU的P22.0引脚（`MCU_VCC0V75_EN`），高电平使能 | 证据[1], [20] | 明确 | | **I²C控制网络** | 连接到MCU的I²C总线（SDA: P15.5, SCL: P15.4），I²C地址0x42 | 证据[13], [20] | 明确 |

Reset the chip with P12.1 pin of the MCU. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F06/F11</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>上电模式、下电模式、运行模式、复位模式、OTA模式 Power on mode, power off mode, run mode, reset mode, OTA mode</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>上电模式 Power on 当唤醒信号有效后，系统一级电源输出VCC3V3电源，MCU开始工作，MCU_UB_SW_P00.10信号输出逻辑高，使能KL30电压采样电路，之后UBSW_ACQ输出分压后的模拟信号，MCU开始通过ADC采样UBSW_ACQ信号。 1. Power on When the wake-up signal becomes active, the system's primary power supply outputs the VCC3V3 voltage, and the MCU starts operating. The MCU_UB_SW_P00.10 signal then outputs a logic high level to enable the KL30 voltage sampling circuit. Subsequently, UBSW_ACQ outputs a divided analog signal, after which the MCU begins ADC sampling of the UBSW_ACQ signal.运行模式 MCU实时采样UBSW_ACQ用于电源管理功能。 2.Run mode MCU real-time sampling UBSW_ACQ is used for power management functions. 下电模式 唤醒信号无效后，MCU在off delay期间将MCU_UB_SW_P00.10信号配置为逻辑低，关闭KL30采样功能，系统下电。 3. Power off After the wake-up signal is invalid, the MCU will configure the MCU_UB_Sw_P00.10 signal to logic low during offdelay, turn off the KL30 sampling function, and power down the system. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td> 参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirements specification. Product system design specification</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><caption> Table 6-1. Function</td><td>ADAS正常工作电压范围9~16VDC，系统需求需要对产品供电KL30进行电源管理，硬件电路对UBD电源网络进行ADC采样。 ADAS normal operating voltage range is 9~16VDC. System requirements require power management for the product power supply KL30. The hardware circuit performs ADC sampling on the UBD power supply.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>MCU_UB_SW_P00.10信号是由MCU I/O控制的数字信号，用于使能KL30电压采样功能。 MCU_UB_SW_P00.10 signal is a digital signal controlled by MCU I/O, used to enable KL30 voltage sampling function. UBSW_ACQ_AN6信号是UBD经过100K 1%、10K 1%电阻分压后的模拟信号。 UBSW_ACQ_AN6 signal is an analog signal of UBD divided by 100K 1%、10K 1% resistors. Which is connected to the ADC pin of MCU for sampling. MCU的ADC采集到UBD后进行电源管理，当软件诊断到UBD电压范围异常时，MCU会关断EQ和其他负载工作电源，确保ADAS功能安全。 The ADC of MCU collects UBD for power management. When the software detects that UBD voltage range is abnormal, the MCU will turn off EQ and other load power supplies to ensure the functional safety of ADAS.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. 问题：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过哪颗负载开关，使能信号是什么？
参考答案：VCC1V8 经 U1803（TPS22918）输出为 VCC1V8_EQ，使能信号为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：根据现有证据，回答如下： ### 子问题1：VCC1V8到EQ的1.8V电源路径上经过的负载开关型号是什么？ **缺失信息**：在提供的证据中，未明确标识出“VCC1V8到EQ的1.8V电源路径”上经过的负载开关型号。证据[1]显示了网络`&1V8_EQ_ACQ`（可能为EQ的1.8V电源）的连接关系，但未提及任何负载开关。其他网络（如`&3V3_EQ_ACQ`、`EQ6L_GPIO_A0_P23.0`等）也未显示与负载开关的关联。因此，**无法确定经过的负载开关型号**。 ### 子问题2：该负载开关的使能信号是什么？ **缺失信息**：由于未找到负载开关的型号，其使能信号同样**无法确定**。所有证据均未提供任何使能信号信息。 ### 缺失信息总结 - **缺失项1**：VCC1V8到EQ的1.8V电源路径上经过的负载开关型号。 - **缺失项2**：该负载开关的使能信号。 **来源说明**：以上分析基于证据[1]至[8]（均来自`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`电路设计文件）。这些证据仅显示了网络连接，未涉及负载开关器件及其控制信号。

问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：好的，根据您提供的证据，我将严格基于检索到的信息回答您的问题。 ### 子问题1：文档中列出了哪些CAN相关模块？ 根据检索到的文档证据，该板卡上声明了以下CAN相关模块： * **CAN0** (证据[1]到[5]中提及的`CAN0H`, `CAN0L`, `CAN0_INH`, `CAN0_INH_ACQ`, `CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8`等网络) * **CAN1** (证据[6]到[8]中提及的`CAN1H`, `CAN1L`, `CAN1_INH`等网络) * **CAN2** (证据[9]到[15]中提及的`CAN2H`, `CAN2L`, `CAN2_INH`, `CAN2_INH_ACQ`, `CAN2_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS3_P00.5`, `MCU_CAN2_RXD_P14.1`, `MCU_CAN2_TXD_P14.0`等网络) * **CAN3** (证据[16]到[20]中提及的`CAN3H`, `CAN3L`, `CAN3_INH`, `CAN3_INH_ACQ`, `CAN3_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS6_P00.8`等网络) **来源说明：** 以上信息来源于电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 中的网表定义。 ###
