Reset the chip with P12.1 pin of the MCU. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | 顾客代表Customer Representative其他与项目相关人员等Other relevant person术语及缩写解释 Terms and AbbreviationsTable 1-1Terms and Abbreviations参考文档 Reference DocumentTable 1-2Reference Document硬件架构 Hardware Architecture硬件架构描述了60060894_ADAS项目的硬件基础模块，模块和模块之间的接口。The Hardware Architecture describe the HW basic component of 600608964_ADAS project, as well as the interface of each component.系统概述 System overviewADAS产品要求功能安全达到ASIL-B，硬件整体架构介绍如下：ADAS products require functional safety to meet ASIL-B. The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table>

实际回答：好的，我将严格依据提供的证据，逐颗列出所有 TPS22918 的输入电源、输出电源和 ON 控制引脚连接。 ### 子问题：TPS22918 数据手册中关于输入电源（VIN）、输出电源（VOUT）和 ON 控制引脚的定义是什么？ 根据提供的电路设计文件和文档文本，共发现 3 颗 TPS22918 器件实例，其输入电源、输出电源和 ON 控制引脚连接如下： **1. 器件实例 U1500** * **输入电源 (VIN):** 根据文档，该器件输入为 **VCC3V3** 电源。 * **来源:** 文档 [17] 中 F17 模块描述：“TPS22918负载开关，VCC3V3输入，VCC3V3_ETH输出”。 * **输出电源 (VOUT):** 根据文档，该器件输出为 **VCC3V3_ETH** 电源。 * **来源:** 文档 [15] 中 F17 模块描述：“TPS22918正常输出VCC3V3_ETH”；文档 [17] 中 F17 模块描述：“TPS22918负载开关，VCC3V3输入，VCC3V3_ETH输出”。 * **ON 控制:** 该器件的 ON 引脚连接至网络 **MCU_3V3_ETH_EN**。 * **来源:** 电路设计证据 [12] 显示，U1500.ON 引脚连接到网络 `MCU_3V3_ETH_EN`。 **2. 器件实例 U1802** * **输入电源 (VIN):** 根据文档，该器件输入为 **VCC3V3** 电源。 * **来源:** 文档 [17] 中 F16 模块描述：“TPS22918负载开关，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC3V3_EQ输出”；文档 [20] 中 F16 模块描述：“将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3_EQ”。 * **输出电源 (VOUT):** 根据文档，该器件输出为 **VCC3V3_EQ** 电源。 * **来源:** 文档 [14] 中 F16 模块描述：“TPS22918正常输出VCC3V3_EQ”；文档 [17] 中 F16 模块描述：“TPS22918负载开关，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC3V3_EQ输出”。 * **ON 控制:** 该器件的 ON 引脚连接至网络 **MCU_3V3_EQ_EN**。 * **来源:** 电路设计证据 [11] 显示，U1802.ON 引脚连接到网络 `MCU_3V3_EQ_EN`。 **3. 器件实例 U1803** * **输入电源 (VIN):** 根据文档，该器件输入为 **VCC1V8** 电源。 * **来源:** 文档 [13] 中 F19 模块描述：“VCC1V8电压输入，VCC1V8_EQ电压输出”；文档 [19] 中 F19 模块描述：“将VCC1V8电源转换为VCC1V8_EQ”。 * **输出电源 (VOUT):** 根据文档，该器件输出为 **VCC1V8_EQ** 电源。 * **来源:** 文档 [13] 中 F19 模块描述：“TPS22918正常输出VCC1V8_EQ”；文档 [19] 中 F19 模块描述：“将VCC1V8电源转换为VCC1V8_EQ”。 * **ON 控制:** 该器件的 ON 引脚连接至网络 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**。 * **来源:** 电路设计证据 [10] 显示，U1803.ON 引脚连接到网络 `MCU_1V8_EQ_EN`。 ### 缺失信息 * **数据手册引脚定义:** 所有证据均未直接提供 TPS22918 数据手册中关于 VIN、VOUT 和 ON 引脚的官方定义（如电压范围、逻辑阈值等）。上述信息均来源于电路设计和功能描述文档，仅能说明这些引脚的连接网络和功能，无法确认其具体的电气参数。 ### 证据冲突说明 检索到的证据中存在冲突，但这些冲突涉及其他字段（如 `lol`, `fsw`），与当前问题（TPS22918 的输入电源、输出电源和 ON 控制引脚连接）无关，因此不影响上述答案。 * **冲突字段 `lol`:** 值 “3 ma”, “5 ma”, “9 ma” 之间的冲突。 * **冲突字段 `fsw`:** 值 “1.5”, “2.25”, “2.5”, “3” 之间的冲突。 * **冲突字段 `vih`:** 值 “2 v” 和 “vin” 之间的冲突。

评分上下文：8/30 段，896/22864 字符；已裁剪：是
hw-v1-circuit-vcc3v3-rolesuccessfailed0.5990.429not_applicable0.5000.5001.000failednot_applicable
问题：VCC3V3 在板上主要给哪些电源器件供电？不要把电阻电容当成主要负载。
参考答案：VCC3V3 是主要 3.3V 电源轨，供给 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600 等 IC；回答应聚焦芯片而非无源器件。
实际回答：根据现有证据，VCC3V3 在板上主要给以下电源器件（LDO、DC-DC 转换器、负载开关、电源管理 IC）供电，已排除电阻、电容、电感、磁珠等无源元件： 1. **系统一级 DC-DC（KL30 to VCC3V3）** - 器件：LN10046（或类似方案，用于将 KL30 转换为 VCC3V3） - 来源：证据 [11] 中“KL30 to VCC3V3”描述，以及证据 [20] 中 F12 模块列名“LN10046，KL30电源转VCC3V3电源”。 2. **PMIC（LP87702）** - 输入：VCC3V3 - 输出：VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 - 来源：证据 [11] 中“VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B))”；证据 [12] 中“LP87702 chip is used to convert VCC3V3 into VCC1V8, VCC1V1 and VCC50”；证据 [18] 和 [20] 中 F13 模块列名。 3. **DC-DC 转换器（TPS62872）** - 输入：VCC3V3 - 输出：VCC0V75（用于 EQ6 内核供电） - 来源：证据 [11] 中“VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B))”；证据 [17] 中“TPS62872 power chip is used to convert VCC3V3 to VCCOV75”；证据 [18] 和 [20] 中 F14 模块列名。 4. **DC-DC 转换器（SA23002）** - 输入：VCC3V3 - 输出：0V75_MIPI_DDR（用于 EQ6 的 IO 口供电） - 来源：证据 [11] 中“VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B))”；证据 [18] 和 [20] 中 F15 模块列名“SA23002，VCC3V3输入，0V75_MIPI_DDR电压输出”。 5. **负载开关（TPS22918）** - 输入：VCC3V3 - 输出：VCC3V3_EQ（用于 EQ6 的 IO 和摄像头供电） - 来源：证据 [11] 中“VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B))”；证据 [16] 中“采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3_EQ”；证据 [18] 和 [20] 中 F16 模块列名。 6. **负载开关（TPS22918）** - 输入：VCC3V3 - 输出：VCC3V3_ETH（用于 ETH 供电） - 来源：证据 [11] 中“VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM)”；证据 [15] 中“采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3-ETH”；证据 [18] 和 [20] 中 F17 模块列名。 7. **MCU 内置 BUCK 电源（VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU）** - 输入：VCC3V3_MCU（来源于 VCC3V3） - 输出：VCC1V25_MCU（用于 MCU 核供电） - 来源：证据 [11] 中“VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB)”；证据 [20] 中 F27 模块列名“VCC3V3_MCU转VCC1V25_MCU电压，由MCU的EVRC进行控制”。 **缺失信息**： - 证据中未明确列出除上述器件外，是否有其他电源管理 IC 直接连接到 VCC3V3 网络。原理图网表（证据 [2]）显示 VCC3V3 还连接了 U1500.VIN、U1600.VCCIN、U1700.VIN 等引脚，但未提供这些器件的型号或功能描述，无法确认它们是否为电源器件或属于其他功能模块。 - 证据覆盖度显示子问题 2（筛选出电源器件）为“部分覆盖”，缺少电路设计或表格类型的直接筛选结果，因此上述列表可能不完整，仅基于文档中明确提及的器件。 **证据冲突**： - 证据 [14] 中 VCC1V25_MCU 的精度标注为“1.2-1.35v”，而同文档另一处（同一证据）标注为“3.3v”，存在冲突。 - 证据 [19] 中功能安全等级（ASIL）标注存在冲突，例如“ASIL-B”与“B”、“QM”等值同时出现，且字段“hw interface component”被赋予不同取值（如“asil”、“b”、“qm”）。 - 证据 [19] 中同一字段“none”被赋予多个不同值（如“1”、“1v25”、“3v3”、“a19”、“b”等），无法确定这些值的含义或归一化关系。 - 其他字段（如“address”、“reset”、“tj”、“en”）在不同证据中取值不一致，但均与问题无关，不影响本题答案。

power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. None：POWER; None：VCC3V3_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：3V3供电，External Power Supply; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | 10.4PowerSupplyRecommendations The TPS6287x-Q1 family has no special requirements for its input power supply. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. This register returns the device status flags. 10 Application and Implementation Note Information in the following applications sections is not part of the Tl component specification, and Tl does not warrant its accuracy or completeness. Tl's customers are responsible for determining suitability of components for their purposes, as well as validating and testing their design implementation to confirm systemfunctionality. 10.1 Application Information The following section discusses selection of the external components to complete the power supply design for typical a application. 10.2 Typical Application 10.2.1 Design Requirements Preliminary Calculations The maximum allowable deviation of the power supply is ±3.3%. The DC accuracy of the TPS6287x-Q1 is specified as ±1%, therefore, the maximum output voltage variation during a transient is given by: VoUT = ± VouT × (3.3% - 1%) = ±17.25 mV 10.2.2 Detailed Design Procedure The following subsections describe how to calculate the external components required to meet the specified transient requirements of a given application. The calculations include the worst-case variation of components and use the RMS method to combine the variation of uncorrelated parameters. 10.2.2.1 Selecting the Inductor The TPS6287x-Q1 devices have been optimized for inductors in the range 50 nH to 300 nH. If the transient response of the converter is limited by the slew rate of the current in the inductor, using a smaller inductor can improve performance. However, the output ripple current increases as the value of the inductor decreases and higher output current ripple generates higher output voltage ripple, which adds to the transient overshoot or undershoot. The optimal configuration for a given application is always a trade-off between a number of parameters. Tl recommends a starting value of 110 nH for typical applications. | None：U20; None：P23.2; None：P23.2/GTM_TIM1_IN6_5/GTM_TIM0_IN6_5/GTM_TOUT43/CAN12_TXD; None：GPIO; None：MCU_RST_FLASH_P23.2; None：/; None：O; None：FLASH复位信号 低：FLASH复位 高：FLASH进入工作状态; None：HI-Z，硬件外部下拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 None：未使用; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：NA; None：/; None：D7; None：P11.14; None：P11.14/GTM_TIM4_IN7_4/GTM_TIM2_IN7_8/GETH_CRSDVB/GETH_RXDVB/GETH_CRSA/GTM_TOUT126; None：GPIO; None：MCU_ETH_CFG1_P11.14; None：/; None：I; None：CFG1：BOM配置，判断是否有以太网 高：TBD 低：TBD; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 None：未使用; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：NA; None：/; None：D6; None：P11.15; None：P11.15/GTM_TIM4_IN7_5/GTM_TIM0_IN7_8/GETH_COLA/GTM_TOUT127; None：GPIO; None：MCU_ETH_CFG2_P11.15; None：/; None：I; None：CFG2：BOM配置，判断以太网接口类型 高：TBD 低：TBD; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 HW Interface Component ：QM(B); None：F13/POWER; None：1、LP87702实现VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50三路电源输出 2、LP87702的VCC50由MCU的GPIO控制使能和关断，VCC1V8、VCC1V1由MCU的I2C控制使能和关断。 3、预留中断信号用于MCU辅助监控LP87702的工作状态。 4、MCU可以通过I2C监控和管理LP87702的状态。; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：B17; None：P15.3; None：P15.3/GTM_TIM3_IN6_4/GTM_TIM2_IN6_4/CAN01_RXDA/QSPI2_SCLKA/GTM_TOUT74/QSPI2_SCLK/MSC0_EN1; None：GPIO; None：MCU_5V_EN; None：/; None：O; None：VCC50（LP87702）电源使能控制信号 高：使能VCC50电压输出 低：关断VCC50电压输出; None：HI-Z，硬件外部下拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><caption> over operating junction temperature (TJ = -40 °C to 150 °C) and Vin = 2.7 V to 6 V. hw-v1-multihop-eth-enablesuccess0.1670.0000.000not_applicable0.0000.0001.0000.000not_applicable
问题：从 MCU 使能开始，追踪到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径。
参考答案：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN 使能 U1500，VCC3V3 经 U1500 形成 VCC3V3_ETH，再进入 U1501 TPS74501，输出 VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：根据现有证据，对“从 MCU 使能开始，追踪到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径”问题，按子问题组织如下： ### 子问题 1：MCU 的使能信号（如 EN 引脚）连接到哪个电源管理芯片或 LDO？ **状态：部分覆盖** * **证据与来源：** 电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 中，网络 `ECU_EN` 连接了电源管理芯片 `U1600` 的 `EN/SYNC` 引脚（证据ID: [11]）。这表明 MCU 发出的使能信号（`ECU_EN`）直接连接到了 `U1600` 的使能引脚。 * **缺失信息：** 未能从现有证据中明确识别出 MCU 上具体哪个 GPIO 引脚产生了 `ECU_EN` 信号。虽然证据中提到了多个 MCU 的 GPIO 引脚（如 `P02.8`, `P00.2` 等），但它们的连接目标（如 CAN 控制器）与 `ECU_EN` 网络无关。因此，MCU 使能信号的起始点（具体从 MCU 哪个引脚输出）无法确定。 ### 子问题 2：该电源管理芯片或 LDO 的输出是否为 1.0V？其输出如何连接到以太网 PHY 的电源引脚？ **状态：未覆盖** * **缺失信息：** * 现有证据未提供电源管理芯片 `U1600` 的输出电压值。没有数据手册或相关文档说明其输出是否为 1.0V。 * 证据中识别到网络 `&1V0_ETH_ACQ`（带“1V0”字样，暗示为 1.0V 电源，证据ID: [18]），但该网络连接至 MCU 的模拟输入引脚（`U900A.AN9`），用于电压采样/监测，而非直接连接到以太网 PHY 的电源引脚。 * 证据中提到的与以太网 PHY `U1400` 直接相连的网络（如 `PHY_MDIN`, `PHY_MDIP`, `PHY_RESET_N_P22.3`, `PHY_WAKE_P21.5`）均为数据或控制信号，不包含明确的 1.0V 电源引脚连接。 * 因此，无法确认 `U1600` 的输出是否为 1.0V，也无法确认其与以太网 PHY 电源引脚之间的直接连接关系。 ### 子问题 3：完整路径中是否有其他中间节点（如滤波电路、磁珠）？ **状态：缺失** * **缺失信息：** 在提供的所有电路设计网表和文档中，均未找到任何关于在 MCU 使能信号、电源管理芯片输出或通向 PHY 的电源路径上存在滤波电路（如电容、电感）或磁珠等中间节点的直接证据。 ### 总结 目前无法构建从 MCU 使能到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径。主要缺口在于： 1. **MCU 使能信号源：** 无法确定 MCU 上产生 `ECU_EN` 的具体引脚。 2. **电源管理芯片输出：** 无法确定 `U1600` 的输出电压是否为 1.0V。 3. **电源路径连接：** 无法确定 `U1600` 的 1.0V 输出如何连接到以太网 PHY 的电源引脚。 4. **中间节点：** 路径中是否存在滤波电路、磁珠等中间节点，无任何证据支持。
